独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 38969420819 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 1

轻微指引缺失上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F4 · 指引缺少任务要求演示数据必须可见时的种子判定示例

文档 / Skill / 示例 · app/AGENTS.md · @nocobase/db · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

候选主张成立：nocobase-db SKILL 与 AGENTS.md 只定义『运行所需数据用必需 seed / 示例内容用 sample:true 且未启用时 skipped』，未给出任务验收依赖记录在运行时可见时如何在必需 seed 与显式开启 sampleData 间判定的示例。初始实现据此把三条 fixture 写成 sample seed（E51），在未设 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 时被 seeder 记为 skipped（E13），修复轮被迫通读 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 配置并查找环境设置（E44/E45）后改判为必需 seed（E23）。补充决策示例的改进空间可由指引文本与已发生误判直接证实。注：原候选引用的 E11 落在 AGENTS.md API documentation 段，不支撑种子结论，正确的 Database 段以新增 E52 佐证。

  • 职责与公开约定：已核对的约定：nocobase-db SKILL §2 规定 seed 写入应用运行所需记录，示例/演示内容不是安装数据、必须声明为 sample seed（E14）；同节说明 sample seed 仅在 app.sampleData/APP_SAMPLE_DATA 启用时运行，否则记 skipped（E15）。AGENTS.md Database 段同样规定『Data the application requires to run is a seed... Sample data is not required data』并把 sample seed 绑定到 APP_SAMPLE_DATA、未设时记 skipped（E52）。两处只给定义，没有把『任务/验收要求记录在运行时可见』映射到具体选择的示例。候选引用的 E11 实际是 AGENTS.md 的 API documentation 段（186-198 行），不是 Database 段，本身不支撑该种子主张。 E14 E15 E52 E11
  • 实际行为证据：已发生的实际行为：implement Agent 的 retro 自述实现为『a `todos` collection (migration + sample seed)』（E51），即初始确把三条待办声明为 sample seed。DefaultSeeder 对 sample===true 且未启用 sampleData 的种子写 status=skipped 并跳过（E13），因此无 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 的运行库为空。修复轮 Agent 在推理中确认『sample seeds only load when APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true』并通读 server/config/app.ts 的变量说明（E44）、遍查 .github 无该变量（E45），最终把同一批记录改判为必需 seed 并在注释写明『required data rather than optional sample data』（E23）。行为与契约一致，且暴露契约中缺少可操作判定示例。 E51 E13 E44 E45 E23
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用侧初始误装配成立：把验收要求运行时可见的过期/未来/已完成三条 fixtures 以 sample:true 声明，导致无 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 时被 seeder 记为 skipped、运行库为空（E51、E13）。修复未改框架，而是把同一记录改判为必需 seed（E23），说明这是应用对指引的误用，而指引未提供可机械判定的示例；因此候选归因于 guidance-gap 而非应用缺陷是合理的。 E23 E51 E13
  • 环境因素：环境材料显示 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 未被启用：应用仅在 server/config/app.ts 暴露 APP_SAMPLE_DATA（E44），而修复 Agent 在 .github/workflows、.github/actions、config.example.yml、.env* 中均未找到设置该变量的位置（E44、E45）。这解释了 sample seed 为何不加载，但正是候选要求指引覆盖的『环境不设 APP_SAMPLE_DATA』前提；环境事实不能推翻『指引缺少判定示例』这一改进空间，也未证明该指引已足够。 E44 E45
  • 工厂因素：工厂是否应负责把任务元数据 sampleData 映射为 APP_SAMPLE_DATA，缺少冻结的工厂运行配置材料：现有材料只表明 workflows/actions 中没有 APP_SAMPLE_DATA（E45），不足以确认或排除工厂注入层是另一主因，故保留未知。这不否定文档层的改进空间，只是不把工厂当作已证实的根因。 E45
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：已存在的公开能力边界：指引本已区分必需 seed 与 sample seed，并给出 sampleDataToken 入口（E14、E15、E52），应用本可自行选择必需 seed。但未发现任何文档示例或入口把『任务/验收依赖记录在运行时存在』映射到具体选择，也未提供可复制的模式；已发生的误判（E51）表明现有文字边界不足以防止该错判，故补充示例确有改进空间。未使用/未发现不作为不存在的直接证据，这里仅作指引改进依据。 E14 E15 E52 E51

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

nocobase-db Skill 与 app/AGENTS.md 都只声明示例/演示内容不是安装数据、须用 sample:true，并说明未启用时记为 skipped；但没有覆盖另一类情形：构建/验收任务明确要求若干条演示记录在运行库中可见，而环境不设 APP_SAMPLE_DATA。代理读完指引后仍把这三行判为 sample，说明规则本身正确但缺少可操作的决策边界。

实际影响
同一误判可能重复发生并造成一整轮返工；补齐决策示例可把该边界变成可机械执行的检查。
建议改法
在 nocobase-db SKILL 的 Migrations and seeds 或 AGENTS.md 的 Database 段落加入一句判定：若任务/验收脚本依赖这些记录在运行时存在，则用必需 seed 或在安装配置中显式开启 sampleData，并给出示例。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
任务要求运行库中存在过期待办/未来待办/已完成待办各 1 条，代理据 Skill 把演示内容声明为 sample:true。
应有能力 / 行为
指引给出判定规则与示例，说明任务要求运行时可见的演示数据应如何声明与加载。
实际观察
指引只规定演示内容用 sample:true 且未启用时 skipped，未说明任务要求可见时该改用必需 seed 或开启 sampleData。
应用绕行与代价
应用侧改用必需 seed；框架未提供自动提示。
改进后的验收标准
nocobase-db Skill 或 AGENTS.md 增加判定示例后，代理能在同一情形下选择必需 seed 或显式开启 sampleData。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 5 条
app/AGENTS.md:186–198E11

指引要求每个 /api 路由声明 tags/summary/operationId，输入用 apiValidator、响应用 listResponse 等；文档在 /api/swagger；并点名 findUndeclaredApiRoutes() 与 findApiDocumentSchemaProblems() 的自检测试。

### API documentation

The running application serves an OpenAPI 3.1 document of every `/api` route it has — its own, every registered plugin's, the data endpoints and Better Auth's `/api/auth/...` — at `<origin><APP_BASE_PATH>/api/swagger` (JSON), with Swagger UI at `<origin><APP_BASE_PATH>/api/swagger/docs`. Locally that is `http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/api/swagger/docs`, with `/main` replaced by the actual `APP_BASE_PATH`.

Reading either needs a signed-in session or an API key: open the Swagger UI in a browser where you are signed in, or send a key in `x-api-key`. A signed-in user creates keys with `POST <APP_BASE_PATH>/api/apiKeys`; the `nocobase-app-plugin-api-keys` Skill covers the plugin. Without a valid credential the routes answer `401` with reason `API_DOCS_UNAUTHENTICATED`, and an application with no access check registered, such as one without the authentication plugin, answers `404`.

```bash
curl -H "x-api-key: <key>" http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/api/swagger
```

**Learn the available endpoints from this document instead of reading route sources.** Before calling an endpoint or building on one, fetch the JSON with a key the user gives you and find the operations by `tags` or `operationId`; read the source only for what the document does not say. Fetch it again after registering a plugin or changing a Collection.

Every `/api` route the application writes declares itself, as in the example above: `tags`, an English `summary` and a unique camelCase `operationId`; input through `apiValidator()`; responses through `dataResponse()`, `listResponse()`, `emptyResponse()` and `apiErrorResponse(status)` for exactly the statuses the route can produce. Do not list `400` for input validation: a route that uses `apiValidator` gets the `400` automatically. List `400` yourself only for another reason, such as a failed precondition. `apiErrorResponses` is `401`, `403` and `500`, for an authenticated route with a permission check; otherwise list each status the route can return with `apiErrorResponse(code)`. Response schemas live in `server/routes/schemas.ts`, typed against what the service returns. A route reached without a credential adds `security: []`. Hide a route with `describeRoute({ hide: true })` and a comment only for the few reasons `references/http-api.md` lists. Data endpoints are documented without a declaration; a field a data exposure adds to every record goes in its `computedFields`. A test starting the application expects `findUndeclaredApiRoutes()` and `findApiDocumentSchemaProblems()` to be empty; `.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md` shows it.

DefaultSeeder.run 对 sample===true 且未启用 sampleData 的种子写入 status=skipped、不执行，并计入 skippedSamples。

            const samplesEnabled = this.options.sample?.enabled === true;
            for (const seed of pending) {
                if (seed.seed.sample === true && !samplesEnabled) {
                    await recordSeedCompleted(seedConnection, {
                        tableName: this.options.tableName,
                        packageName: seed.packageName,
                        name: seed.name,
                        checksum: seed.checksum,
                        durationMs: null,
                        status: 'skipped',
                    });
                    skippedSamples.push(seed.name);
                    continue;
                }
                await this.runSeed(connection, seed);
                executed.push(seed.name);
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:86–95E14

migration 建结构、seed 写运行所需数据；示例或演示内容不是安装数据，必须声明为 sample seed 而不能当普通 seed。

## 2. Migrations and seeds

A migration creates structure. A seed inserts the records the application needs in order to run. Neither does the other's job. Sample or demonstration content is not install data: declare it as a sample seed, described below, never as a plain seed.

```ts
// database/main/migrations/202609020001_create_orders.ts
import { defineMigration, type MigrationDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({
  name: '202609020001_create_orders',
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:147–147E15

sample seed 仅在安装时 app.sampleData=true 时运行，否则记为 skipped；pnpm nocobase db sample 可在开发中补跑。

Sample data goes in the same `seeds` directory, declared with `defineSeed({ name, sample: true, run })`. It runs only when the application installs its database with `app.sampleData` set (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`): the connection held no migration or seed history before that start, or `db reset` rebuilt it. Otherwise the seed is recorded as skipped and never runs on its own; `pnpm nocobase db sample` runs every skipped sample seed in development, and a deployment refuses it. Sample data that has to go through other plugins' services, such as projects with issues, is registered instead on `sampleDataToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/sample-data`, from a service provider's `boot()`: `container.resolve(sampleDataToken).register({ name, packageName, run })`. It runs once the application is ready, under the same condition, and is recorded in the default connection's seed history as `sample-data:<name>`. The seed history's `status` column, `executed` or `skipped`, records which happened; the library adds it to an existing history table on the next run.
app/database/main/seeds/202610070001_default_todos.ts:1–61E23

必需种子幂等插入过期/未来/已完成各 1 条，expired 初始为 false，按 title 去重；注释说明 sample:true 会被记录为 skipped。

import { defineSeed, type SeedDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';

/** The subset of a `todos` row this seed writes. */
interface TodoRow {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  dueAt: string | Date;
  completed: boolean;
  expired: boolean;
}

const DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

/**
 * The three todos every installation starts with: one already past its due time, one far in the future, and one
 * finished. They are the fixture the `todos.check-expired` schedule is observed against, so they are required data
 * rather than optional sample data: with `sample: true` the seeder records itself as skipped when the database is
 * installed without `APP_SAMPLE_DATA`, and the running application then has nothing to mark or to leave alone.
 *
 * `expired` starts `false` on every row. Pre-setting the flag would leave nothing for the schedule to show; the
 * schedule's own run is the only thing that may flip it. The insert is skipped when a todo with the same title already
 * exists, so a repeated `pnpm nocobase db apply` never adds a duplicate.
 */
const seed: SeedDefinition = defineSeed({
  name: '202610070001_default_todos',

  async run(context) {
    const todos = context.repository<TodoRow>('todos');
    const samples: Pick<
      TodoRow,
      'title' | 'dueAt' | 'completed' | 'expired'
    >[] = [
      {
        title: '已过期待办',
        dueAt: new Date(Date.now() - DAY_MS),
        completed: false,
        expired: false,
      },
      {
        title: '未来待办',
        dueAt: new Date(Date.now() + 3650 * DAY_MS),
        completed: false,
        expired: false,
      },
      {
        title: '已完成待办',
        dueAt: new Date(Date.now() - DAY_MS),
        completed: true,
        expired: false,
      },
    ];

    for (const sample of samples) {
      if (await todos.exists({ filter: { title: sample.title } })) continue;
      await todos.createOne({ values: sample });
    }
  },
});

export default seed;

### [轻微][指引缺失] 指引缺少任务要求演示数据必须可见时的种子判定示例

**相关对象**：`app/AGENTS.md`、`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.18）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

候选主张成立：nocobase-db SKILL 与 AGENTS.md 只定义『运行所需数据用必需 seed / 示例内容用 sample:true 且未启用时 skipped』，未给出任务验收依赖记录在运行时可见时如何在必需 seed 与显式开启 sampleData 间判定的示例。初始实现据此把三条 fixture 写成 sample seed（E51），在未设 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 时被 seeder 记为 skipped（E13），修复轮被迫通读 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 配置并查找环境设置（E44/E45）后改判为必需 seed（E23）。补充决策示例的改进空间可由指引文本与已发生误判直接证实。注：原候选引用的 E11 落在 AGENTS.md API documentation 段，不支撑种子结论，正确的 Database 段以新增 E52 佐证。

- 职责与公开约定：已核对的约定：nocobase-db SKILL §2 规定 seed 写入应用运行所需记录，示例/演示内容不是安装数据、必须声明为 sample seed（E14）；同节说明 sample seed 仅在 app.sampleData/APP_SAMPLE_DATA 启用时运行，否则记 skipped（E15）。AGENTS.md Database 段同样规定『Data the application requires to run is a seed... - 实际行为证据：已发生的实际行为：implement Agent 的 retro 自述实现为『a `todos` collection (migration + sample seed)』（E51），即初始确把三条待办声明为 sample seed。DefaultSeeder 对 sample===true 且未启用 sampleData 的种子写 status=skipped 并跳过（E13），因此无 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 的运行库为空。修复轮 Agent 在推理中确认『sample seeds only load when APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true』并通读 server/config/app.ts 的变量说明（E44）、遍查 .github 无该变量（E45），最终把同一批记录改判为必需 seed 并在注释写明『required data rather than optional sample data』（E23）。行为与契约一致，且暴露契约中缺少可操作判定示例。；证据：E51（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0035.txt:1425–1425）、E13（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/seed/seeder.js:65–80）、E44（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-2.jsonl/part-0001.txt:970–980）、E45（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-2.jsonl/part-0001.txt:1040–1050）、E23（app/database/main/seeds/202610070001_default_todos.ts:1–61）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用侧初始误装配成立：把验收要求运行时可见的过期/未来/已完成三条 fixtures 以 sample:true 声明，导致无 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 时被 seeder 记为 skipped、运行库为空（E51、E13）。修复未改框架，而是把同一记录改判为必需 seed（E23），说明这是应用对指引的误用，而指引未提供可机械判定的示例；因此候选归因于 guidance-gap 而非应用缺陷是合理的。；证据：E23（app/database/main/seeds/202610070001_default_todos.ts:1–61）、E51（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0035.txt:1425–1425）、E13（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/seed/seeder.js:65–80）
- 环境因素：环境材料显示 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 未被启用：应用仅在 server/config/app.ts 暴露 APP_SAMPLE_DATA（E44），而修复 Agent 在 .github/workflows、.github/actions、config.example.yml、.env* 中均未找到设置该变量的位置（E44、E45）。这解释了 sample seed 为何不加载，但正是候选要求指引覆盖的『环境不设 APP_SAMPLE_DATA』前提；环境事实不能推翻『指引缺少判定示例』这一改进空间，也未证明该指引已足够。；证据：E44（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-2.jsonl/part-0001.txt:970–980）、E45（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-2.jsonl/part-0001.txt:1040–1050）
- 工厂因素：工厂是否应负责把任务元数据 sampleData 映射为 APP_SAMPLE_DATA，缺少冻结的工厂运行配置材料：现有材料只表明 workflows/actions 中没有 APP_SAMPLE_DATA（E45），不足以确认或排除工厂注入层是另一主因，故保留未知。这不否定文档层的改进空间，只是不把工厂当作已证实的根因。；证据：E45（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-2.jsonl/part-0001.txt:1040–1050）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：已存在的公开能力边界：指引本已区分必需 seed 与 sample seed，并给出 sampleDataToken 入口（E14、E15、E52），应用本可自行选择必需 seed。但未发现任何文档示例或入口把『任务/验收依赖记录在运行时存在』映射到具体选择，也未提供可复制的模式；已发生的误判（E51）表明现有文字边界不足以防止该错判，故补充示例确有改进空间。未使用/未发现不作为不存在的直接证据，这里仅作指引改进依据。；证据：E14（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:86–95）、E15（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:147–147）、E52（app/AGENTS.md:200–202）、E51（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0035.txt:1425–1425）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

nocobase-db Skill 与 app/AGENTS.md 都只声明示例/演示内容不是安装数据、须用 sample:true，并说明未启用时记为 skipped；但没有覆盖另一类情形：构建/验收任务明确要求若干条演示记录在运行库中可见，而环境不设 APP_SAMPLE_DATA。代理读完指引后仍把这三行判为 sample，说明规则本身正确但缺少可操作的决策边界。

**影响**：同一误判可能重复发生并造成一整轮返工；补齐决策示例可把该边界变成可机械执行的检查。

**建议改动**：在 nocobase-db SKILL 的 Migrations and seeds 或 AGENTS.md 的 Database 段落加入一句判定：若任务/验收脚本依赖这些记录在运行时存在，则用必需 seed 或在安装配置中显式开启 sampleData，并给出示例。

**验收标准**：nocobase-db Skill 或 AGENTS.md 增加判定示例后，代理能在同一情形下选择必需 seed 或显式开启 sampleData。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/AGENTS.md:186–198` — 指引要求每个 /api 路由声明 tags/summary/operationId，输入用 apiValidator、响应用 listResponse 等；文档在 /api/swagger；并点名 findUndeclaredApiRoutes() 与 findApiDocumentSchemaProblems() 的自检测试。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/seed/seeder.js:65–80` — DefaultSeeder.run 对 sample===true 且未启用 sampleData 的种子写入 status=skipped、不执行，并计入 skippedSamples。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:86–95` — migration 建结构、seed 写运行所需数据；示例或演示内容不是安装数据，必须声明为 sample seed 而不能当普通 seed。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:147–147` — sample seed 仅在安装时 app.sampleData=true 时运行，否则记为 skipped；pnpm nocobase db sample 可在开发中补跑。
- `app/database/main/seeds/202610070001_default_todos.ts:1–61` — 必需种子幂等插入过期/未来/已完成各 1 条，expired 初始为 false，按 title 去重；注释说明 sample:true 会被记录为 skipped。

Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/db

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

首轮种子使用 defineSeed sample:true，在未设置 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 的 QA 环境中被 seeder 记录 status=skipped 而不执行，verify-2 观察到的 Todos 页为 No todos，且缺少设置页/启停控件。repair-2 将其改为必需种子（去掉 sample:true）并按 title 幂等去重，同时补上设置页，verify-3 三项通过。

实际影响

建议改法
对任务/验收脚本要求运行时可见的数据一律使用必需 seed；仅在纯演示且环境显式开启 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 时使用 sample:true。

F6 · 验收措辞假设模板存在既有设置页，而模板 shell 默认没有设置区

工厂流程 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

B03 要求「在既有设置页停用计划」，需求也称沿用原有设置；但 nocobase-app-development Skill 明确要求保留单一 App shell 且没有独立 settings area，verify-2 也确认模板账户菜单无设置入口、/main/settings 回主页。应用最终自建了普通侧边栏 Settings 页面才满足该项。

措辞与模板能力不一致会让代理在「沿用既有设置」与「新建页面」之间取舍，增加返工与误判风险。

在验收/需求文本中注明模板无独立设置页，约定以普通受路由保护的设置页面承载，或在模板中提供可复用的设置入口。

应有能力 / 行为

实际观察
模板 shell 按设计没有设置区，应用必须新建 /settings 页面才能满足停用计划的要求。
应用绕行与代价
应用新增 Settings 页面复用 Scheduler 授权与启停端点。
改进后的验收标准
任务/验收文本注明模板无设置页时的处理方式，或模板提供可复用设置入口。

F5 · 代理在复盘里断言 API 文档自检 helper 未被安装包导出，实际由 @nocobase/app-server/router 正常导出

Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/app-server · app/AGENTS.md

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

代理在 implement 阶段写入的复盘称 findUndeclaredApiRoutes() 与 findApiDocumentSchemaProblems() 在整个已安装的 @nocobase/app-testing / @nocobase/app-server 中不存在，并据此把它作为文档缺陷上报。实测两个函数分别由 openapi/document.js 与 openapi/schema.js 导出，并经 @nocobase/app-server/router 的 openapi/index 再导出；代理的 grep 命中失败源于它探测的路径 node_modules/@nocobase/app-server/dist/server/router/*.d.ts 与真实的 dist/router 布局不符。

评审时 · 文档 / API 声称

findUndeclaredApiRoutes() 与 findApiDocumentSchemaProblems() 不属于已安装包的导出，AGENTS.md 引用了不存在的能力。

评审时 · 实际观察

两个函数在 @nocobase/app-server/dist/router/openapi/document.js 与 schema.js 中实现并导出，并被 @nocobase/app-server/router 公开再导出。

实际影响
若不纠正，会把正确的框架指引误判为缺失能力，误导后续修复与评审。
建议改法
以包声明的 imports/exports 或真实 dist 路径核对导出，再下缺失结论；在文档评审中把没有最小复现的缺失声明标为待证。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
代理需要确认 AGENTS.md 提到的 API 文档自检 helper 是否存在。
应有能力 / 行为
按包公开入口或真实 dist 路径核对导出后再下结论。
实际观察
代理以错误的 dist/server/router 路径 grep 失败，得出 helper 未导出的结论并上报为缺陷。
应用绕行与代价
改为从 @nocobase/app-server/router 的命名导出或 openapi/index.d.ts 核对。
改进后的验收标准
评审以实际导出为准推翻该声明，并要求缺失类结论附带最小复现。
E9 E10 E11 E12 E51
实现者过程与运行背景 · 1 个过程问题 / 2 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

Implemented the scheduling feature in the NocoBase 3 application: a `todos` collection (migration + seed), a `TodoService`/`TodoProvider` registering the `todos.check-expired` schedule and its `app.checkExpiredTodos` target with the Scheduler plugin, an authenticated `GET /api/todos` route, and `/todos`, `/schedules` and `/settings` pages with page-level authorization and zh-CN/en-US copy. Verified with a real short-cron Scheduler run in a createAppTest: only the overdue, unfinished todo is marked expired, repeated sweeps add nothing, and an occurrence carries resultSummary.updatedCount. Repair round 2 fixed three QA-observed defects. (1) The three fixture todos were a `sample: true` seed, recorded skipped whenever the database is installed without `APP_SAMPLE_DATA`, so the running app had no todos; it is now a required, idempotent `202610070001_default_todos` seed that `pnpm nocobase db apply` always runs (the required seed is pending by name on an already-installed database, so it executes on the next apply). (2) That empty list was also why 'only overdue unfinished is marked' and 'repeated runs do not duplicate' could not be observed; the existing createAppTest now asserts the three required titles load with no sample data configured. (3) The Schedules page's status was a read-only badge and there was no settings route: added a shadcn `switch` that writes through the Scheduler's own `/enable` and `/disable` endpoints, an AlertDialog confirmation for the destructive disable (I2) with immediate apply and a toast for enable (T4.3), and an ordinary `/settings` sidebar page reusing the same `useSchedules` hook and control under the `page:scheduler.schedules/access` grant.

问题 01 · 修复阶段

Fixture todos were declared as sample data, so the running app had none

本任务已处理

Browser acceptance found zero todos on `/todos`, so the `todos.check-expired` rule ('only overdue unfinished todos become expired') and its idempotence could not be observed, and duplicate execution could not be validated.

原因（实现者自述）
`database/main/seeds/202610070001_sample_todos.ts` declared the three fixtures with `sample: true`. A sample seed is recorded as `skipped` and never loaded unless the database is installed with `app.sampleData` (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`); the factory's verification installs with `db apply` and never sets that variable, and `db apply` records the sample seed as skipped rather than executing it.
处理方式
Deleted the sample seed and added a required, idempotent seed `database/main/seeds/202610070001_default_todos.ts` (not `sample: true`) that inserts one overdue-unfinished, one far-future and one completed todo with `expired = false`, skipping any title that already exists. Re-ran `db apply` against a copy of the verification database: it executed the new seed once, inserted three rows, and a second run inserted none. The `createAppTest` suite now loads the three todos with no `app.sampleData` configured.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · Skill 与文档

AGENTS.md points at API-document test helpers that this version does not export

可自动化

The application development guidance says a test starting the application expects `findUndeclaredApiRoutes()` and `findApiDocumentSchemaProblems()` to be empty, but neither name exists in the installed `@nocobase/app-testing` / `@nocobase/app-server` packages (`grep -r findUndeclaredApiRoutes node_modules/@nocobase` returns nothing). An agent that trusts the text wastes a search and cannot satisfy the stated expectation; the only available substitute is inspecting the served /api/swagger document manually.

建议改动

Either ship those exported helpers in `@nocobase/app-testing` and reference them by their real import path, or reword the guidance to describe the supported check (asserting `describeRoute()` coverage by fetching /api/swagger) so the documented API matches the installed version.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 02 · 脚手架默认值

Required fixture data is easy to declare as sample data and then never ship

可自动化

A task that asks for example data to observe a scheduled effect can be satisfied by either a required seed or a `sample: true` seed, but only the required seed loads in the default install: the factory installs with `pnpm nocobase db apply` and never sets `APP_SAMPLE_DATA`, so a sample seed is recorded skipped and the application starts empty. The distinction is documented, yet the failure is silent until browser acceptance observes missing data.

建议改动

Make the seed helper warn (or fail a strict check) when a `sample: true` seed is the only source of a collection's rows at install time, or surface skipped sample seeds in the `db apply` summary so a reviewer sees that the application installed without them.

来源：Agent · retro.json

原始搭建历史覆盖

输入覆盖：完整纳入已捕获记录 · 原始记录 93.82 MiB · 脱敏后纳入 93.81 MiB

原事件引用覆盖：5/5 次调用 · 显式错误信号核对：42/42

输入完整、引用覆盖和评审推理是不同维度。文件存在不证明 Agent 阅读过；错误信号不等于框架缺陷，已引用也不证明通读或理解了每一字节。

未提供额外说明。

评审源产物：Run 38969420819 / attempt 1。发布重试不改变评审源产物编号。

本次搭建的小型定时任务能力由 NocoBase3 框架直接支撑：Scheduler 插件提供计划定义、目标注册、真实调度与独立执行台账，应用在 Provider boot() 注册 app.checkExpiredTodos 目标与 todos.check-expired 计划；verify-3 三项浏览器验收全部通过，重复执行不新增记录，启用/停用经插件 API 持久保存。@nocobase/app-server 的路由声明、apiValidator、响应/错误 helper 与 OpenAPI 文档自检链完整，应用用法与指引一致。@nocobase/db 的迁移与种子机制把结构变更与运行所需数据分开，但构建/验收要求的演示种子在首轮被声明为 sample:true，未设 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 时被记录为 skipped，导致 verify-2 运行时待办为空，repair-2 改为必需种子后通过；该错误属应用侧误判，指引本身正确，但缺少任务要求演示数据必须可见时的判定示例。另有两点跨切面观察：Scheduler 插件按设计不提供任何页面，应用必须自建计划列表/执行记录/启停 UI；模板 shell 默认没有独立设置区，因此验收措辞中的既有设置页实际需应用新建。评审模型无法渲染 PNG，UI 未做视觉评审。

M1 Scheduler 计划定义、目标注册与实际执行对象、需求覆盖、五项理由与证据

以 @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler 为核心，覆盖 defineSchedule/registerTarget、dispatch 与 occurrence 台账、执行记录与启停 API，以及随包分发的 scheduler Skill 指引。

插件@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler1.0.0-beta.16

schedulerServiceToken / SchedulerService.defineSchedule · SchedulerService.registerTarget · GET /api/scheduler/schedules · GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:id/occurrences · POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:id/enable|disable · ScheduleTargetRegistry · ScheduleOccurrenceStore

E1 E2 E3 E4 E5 E6
指引 / Skillapp/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md版本见冻结输入

Decide Whether to Use Scheduler · Development Loop 的 defineSchedule 步骤 · targets 文档的 registerTarget 扩展面

E7

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

提供一个可在 Scheduler 界面查看、可启停的检查过期待办计划直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
Scheduler 拥有计划定义、触发历史与执行状态；目标和业务逻辑由应用提供。
推荐使用方式
在宿主的 Provider boot() 中调用 schedulerServiceToken.defineSchedule 并以稳定 key 注册；registerTarget 提供目标类型。
Agent 实际使用
应用在 TodoProvider.boot() 注册 app.checkExpiredTodos 目标并定义 todos.check-expired 六段 cron 计划，Schedules 与 Settings 页列出该计划。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E1 E4 E5 E7 E20 E26 E27
必须是真实调度执行并写入执行记录，不能用 setInterval、页面打开或直接改库冒充直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
dispatch 把每次 firing 交给目标并把结果写入 occurrence 台账。
推荐使用方式
目标返回执行状态，由 Scheduler 记录结果；不要自行写 schedule 表。
Agent 实际使用
verify-2 已观察到约每 10 秒一次的真实 occurrence 且状态 Succeeded，verify-3 观察到 Marked 1/0 todo(s) expired 的真实结果。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E2 E3 E5 E20 E27 E28
重复执行同一目标不能新增重复待办，且只标记到期且未完成的记录正常组合
框架职责与业务边界
occurrence 以 jobId 去重；业务幂等由应用目标自行保证。
推荐使用方式
目标内部用稳定过滤条件保证可重复执行；Scheduler 只保证同一次 firing 不被重复执行。
Agent 实际使用
expireOverdue 同时过滤 completed=false、expired=false、dueAt<now，重复执行更新 0 行；occurrence.start 对同一 firing 返回 noop。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E2 E3 E7 E19 E27
计划启用状态要真实持久保存并可核对直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
Scheduler 通过 enable/disable 端点把状态写入计划存储，引擎读取同一处。
推荐使用方式
调用 POST /schedules/:id/enable|disable，不要只改页面状态。
Agent 实际使用
设置页开关调用插件端点；verify-3 停用并刷新后设置页与 Schedules 页均保持 Disabled。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E4 E5 E7 E24 E25 E27

实现完整性与可靠性

88/100

dispatch 用 jobId 作为 occurrenceId 并在终态返回 noop，失败也落台账；路由有标准校验与错误体。

E2 E3 E5

E11

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

自有业务路由按统一契约声明并生成文档直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
app-server 提供路由声明、入参校验、响应体与 OpenAPI 文档生成。
推荐使用方式
用 defineApiRoutes + describeRoute 声明 tags/summary/operationId，输入用 apiValidator，输出用 listResponse 等。
Agent 实际使用
应用的 /api/todos 用 defineApiRoutes 声明、listResponse+apiErrorResponses 声明响应，并复用标准错误体。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E8 E11 E12 E21
错误以稳定的错误码与状态表达，客户端可分支处理直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
apiErrorHandler/apiErrorResponse 表达 domain+reason 的错误体。
推荐使用方式
定义应用级 error domain 与 reason，并由 apiErrorHandler 统一渲染。
Agent 实际使用
应用定义 TODO_ERROR_DOMAIN=todos 与 TODO_ACCESS_REQUIRED，并在子路由上挂 apiErrorHandler。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E8 E21
从运行中的 OpenAPI 文档发现端点，而不是只读路由源码尚未确认
框架职责与业务边界
运行应用在 /api/swagger 提供 3.1 文档与 Swagger UI，自检 helper 检测未声明路由与坏 schema。
推荐使用方式
用带凭证的请求拉取 JSON 并按 tags/operationId 查找，测试断言 findUndeclaredApiRoutes 与 findApiDocumentSchemaProblems 为空。
Agent 实际使用
本轮代理没有使用运行期文档，且曾因路径探测失败误判 helper 不存在；未见其运行自检测试。
缺口归因
归因待确认

E13 E14 E22

框架职责与业务边界
运行所需数据由必需 seed 写入；纯样例内容应声明为 sample。
推荐使用方式
应用运行所需数据用普通 defineSeed；纯样例内容用 sample:true，或在需要时确保加载。
Agent 实际使用
首轮把这三行声明为 sample:true，未设 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 时被记录 skipped，verify-2 运行库为空；repair-2 改为必需 seed 后 verify-3 可见 3 条。
缺口归因

E13 E15 E23 E27 E28

框架职责与业务边界
seed 必须按稳定业务值幂等。
推荐使用方式
用 upsertOne 或按唯一业务键去重。
Agent 实际使用
种子按 title 存在则跳过，重复 db apply 不会新增记录；verify-3 重复执行后总数仍为 3。

未记录能力缺口

业务首轮 QA未验证业务最终轮 QA报告通过

业务验证背景 · 不计入框架评分
本 Run 验证轮次3
本 Run 工厂修复轮次2
已保留验证轮次2

仅本 Run 已归档记录；工厂修复轮次不等于 Agent 开发中全部试错次数。缺少 verify-1 时不能声称任务首轮通过。

首轮与修复过程 · 查看逐轮原始状态
轮次范围原始结论失败项
验证 2全量浏览器 QA报告未通过B01 启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。
B02 再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。
B03 在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。
验证 3全量浏览器 QA报告通过未记录 failed 项；不代表全部操作完成

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 2

做得好的地方
F1 · Scheduler 的目标注册与 occurrence 台账让重复投递只执行一次，并留下可核对的执行记录

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md

⌄

dispatch 以计划 id 作为 job 名、以 context.jobId 作为 occurrenceId，ScheduleOccurrenceStore.start 对同一 firing 返回 noop，因此重复投递不会重复执行业务；目标返回 completed/failed/skipped 都会写入独立执行台账。应用只需提供自幂等的业务目标即可满足不新增重复记录与可核对执行结果。

提供的帮助
本次需求中重复执行仍保持 3 条待办且状态正确，验证所需执行记录由框架产生而非应用伪造，降低了以直接写库冒充调度的风险。
应保留的能力
保持现状；在应用目标中继续把业务幂等过滤写在 Service 内，如 expireOverdue 同时排除 completed 与 expired。
E2 E3 E4 E7 E27 E28
做得好的地方
F2 · 路由声明、入参校验、统一错误体与 OpenAPI 文档自检构成完整可验证链

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-server · app/AGENTS.md

⌄

@nocobase/app-server/router 同时导出 defineApiRoutes、describeRoute、apiValidator、listResponse/dataResponse/emptyResponse、apiErrorResponse(s)/apiErrorHandler、findUndeclaredApiRoutes、findApiDocumentSchemaProblems 与 generateApiDocument。apiValidator 在抛错的同时挂载 JSON Schema/OpenAPI schema，使校验与文档同源；文档自检把未声明路由与坏 $ref 变成可机器断言的空数组。

提供的帮助
应用路由无需自造约定即可被文档化与校验，评审可据导出与自检 helper 核对，而不是只信业务代码自述。
应保留的能力
在应用测试中补一条自检用例，断言 findUndeclaredApiRoutes 与 findApiDocumentSchemaProblems 为空，把该能力纳入常规验证。

问题、误导与建议统一收录在问题与改进，不在此重复展示。

本次评审的覆盖限制
  • 评审模型无法渲染 PNG（读取截图返回 Current model does not support images），因此没有任何页面进入视觉评审；UI 结论仅基于 verify-2/verify-3 报告的文本与截图清单。
  • 受只读约束，未在快照内启动应用、执行测试或复现调度；运行时结论来自 verify-2/verify-3 报告文本与源码交叉核对，未独立重放。
  • 只覆盖本次搭建直接涉及的 4 个能力单元（Scheduler、server 路由与 API 文档、db 迁移与种子、client 壳与导航），未通读全部 34 个已安装包，其他包是否被代理依赖不作结论。
  • historyReview 覆盖 index.json 列出的 5 次调用与全部 42 个错误信号，但错误信号的处置仅基于对应原始事件上下文，未逐条在快照内复现。
  • 缺少 verify-1；过程记录显示共 3 轮验收、2 轮修复，不能据此声称任务首轮即通过，也不代表开发过程中的全部试错。
  • 三个测试文件的实现细节未逐一评审；仅通过 verify-3 报告确认验收项通过，未验证测试断言强度。
  • 快照未包含 3 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。

E1 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/schedules/registry.js:1–49

ScheduleTargetRegistry 以 type 为键注册目标；register 拒绝重复 type；validate/describe/start 都按 type 查目标，未注册返回 target-not-found 或 failed。

export class ScheduleTargetRegistry {
    targets = new Map();
    register(target) {
        if (this.targets.has(target.type))
            throw new Error(`Schedule target type already registered: ${target.type}`);
        // The registry is heterogeneous: each target parses its own config out of
        // the JSON a definition declared, which `validate()` is there to check.
        this.targets.set(target.type, target);
    }
    get(type) {
        return this.targets.get(type);
    }
    validate(definition) {
        const target = this.get(definition.target.type);
        return target
            ? target.validate(definition.target.config)
            : { valid: false, reason: 'target-not-found' };
    }
    async describe(type, config) {
        const target = this.get(type);
        if (!target)
            return { targetLabel: type, state: 'missing' };
        return target.describe
            ? target.describe(config)
            : { targetLabel: target.title, state: 'ready' };
    }
    async start(type, config, context) {
        const target = this.get(type);
        if (!target)
            return { state: 'failed', reason: 'target-not-found' };
        const validation = target.validate(config);
        if (!validation.valid)
            return {
                state: 'failed',
                reason: validation.reason ?? 'invalid-config',
            };
        return target.start(config, context);
    }
    inspect(targetType, reference) {
        const target = this.get(targetType);
        return target?.inspect
            ? target.inspect(reference)
            : Promise.resolve({ state: 'unknown', reason: 'observer-unavailable' });
    }
    referenceHref(targetType, reference) {
        return this.get(targetType)?.referenceHref?.(reference);
    }
}
//# sourceMappingURL=registry.js.map

E2 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/dispatch.js:6–45

createScheduleDispatchJob 以计划 id 作为 job 名；dispatch 用 context.jobId 作为 occurrenceId，occurrences.start 返回 noop 时直接返回，因此同一 firing 被重复投递也只执行一次，结果经 succeed/fail/skip 落台账。

export function createScheduleDispatchJob(spec, limit, dependencies) {
    const options = {
        cron: spec.cron,
        tz: spec.timezone,
        // A schedule's `from` is inclusive, while the executor fires strictly
        // after its start date, as BullMQ does.
        ...(spec.from ? { startDate: new Date(spec.from.getTime() - 1) } : {}),
        ...(spec.to ? { endDate: spec.to } : {}),
        ...(limit !== undefined ? { limit } : {}),
    };
    return {
        name: spec.id,
        options,
        payload: { target: spec.target, definitionHash: spec.definitionHash },
        execute: (context) => dispatch(spec, context, dependencies),
    };
}
async function dispatch(spec, context, { targets, occurrences }) {
    // The occurrence is the firing: its id is the executor's job id, so the same
    // firing delivered twice records, and starts its target, once.
    const occurrenceId = context.jobId;
    const executionContext = { scheduleId: spec.id, occurrenceId };
    const record = {
        scheduleId: spec.id,
        definitionHash: spec.definitionHash,
        targetType: spec.target.type,
        scheduledAt: context.scheduledAt,
    };
    const targetState = (await targets.describe(spec.target.type, spec.target.config)).state;
    if (targetState && targetState !== 'ready') {
        await occurrences.skip(occurrenceId, `target-${targetState}`, record);
        return;
    }
    const action = await occurrences.start(executionContext, spec.definitionHash, spec.target.type, context.scheduledAt);
    if (action === 'noop')
        return;
    try {
        const result = await targets.start(spec.target.type, spec.target.config, executionContext);
        switch (result.state) {
            case 'completed':

原文件 SHA-256：2f586836bd5eba506dcc3a01a7bbcb86c184e779712d5ea6c12bf27bb9cb2495

E3 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/occurrences.js:1–40

定义终态集合 TERMINAL 与 ScheduleOccurrenceStore；start() 对已处于 waiting 或终态的 occurrence 返回 noop，执行状态独立记录。

const TERMINAL = new Set([
    'succeeded',
    'failed',
    'skipped',
    'cancelled',
    'timed_out',
    'triggered',
]);
const MAX_SUMMARY_BYTES = 16_384;
export class ScheduleOccurrenceError extends Error {
    code;
    constructor(code, message) {
        super(message);
        this.code = code;
        this.name = 'ScheduleOccurrenceError';
    }
}
export class ScheduleOccurrenceStore {
    database;
    constructor(database) {
        this.database = database;
    }
    async start(context, definitionHash, targetType, scheduledAt) {
        const now = new Date();
        const existing = await this.find(context.occurrenceId);
        if (existing) {
            if (existing.status === 'waiting' || TERMINAL.has(existing.status))
                return 'noop';
            const updated = await this.database
                .query()
                .updateTable('schedule_occurrences')
                .set({
                status: 'running',
                executionCount: existing.executionCount + 1,
                lastStartedAt: now,
                updatedAt: now,
            })
                .where('id', '=', context.occurrenceId)
                .where('status', 'in', ['pending', 'running'])
                .execute();

E4 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/services/scheduler.js:14–60

DefaultSchedulerService 暴露 defineSchedule、registerTarget、list（带 targetState）、setEnabled，并以 schedulerServiceToken 作为服务标识供应用解析。

    defineSchedule(definition) {
        this.manifest.push(Object.freeze({ definition: buildScheduleDefinition(definition) }));
    }
    registerTarget(target) {
        this.targets.register(target);
        const { type } = target;
        return {
            type,
            reportCompletion: (occurrenceId, reference, completion) => this.occurrences.complete(occurrenceId, reference, completion, type),
        };
    }
    async list() {
        const [records, targets] = await Promise.all([
            this.store.list(),
            this.store.listTargets(),
        ]);
        const byId = new Map(targets.map((target) => [target.id, target]));
        return Promise.all(records.map(async (record) => {
            const target = byId.get(record.id);
            return {
                ...record,
                targetState: target
                    ? ((await this.targets.describe(target.type, target.config))
                        .state ?? 'invalid')
                    : 'missing',
                targetSummary: target
                    ? await this.targets.describe(target.type, target.config)
                    : { targetLabel: record.targetType, state: 'missing' },
            };
        }));
    }
    /** One schedule with the same projection as `list()`, or `undefined` when this application has no such schedule. */
    async get(id) {
        return (await this.list()).find((entry) => entry.id === id);
    }
    async listOccurrences(scheduleId, range = {}) {
        const occurrences = await this.store.listOccurrences(scheduleId, range);
        return occurrences.map((occurrence) => {
            const href = occurrence.target.reference
                ? this.targets.referenceHref(occurrence.target.type, occurrence.target.reference)
                : undefined;
            return {
                ...occurrence,
                target: { ...occurrence.target, ...(href ? { href } : {}) },
            };
        });
    }

/**
 * Registers the plugin's locales, which hold the titles its server registers with the authorization catalog. It
 * contributes no pages: an application that lists schedules builds the page on the scheduler API among its own routes.

E7 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:12–47

Skill 说明使用 Scheduler 的首要理由是让管理员查看执行记录；须在 Provider 中 registerTarget 且业务要自设幂等；只有启停管理 API、没有创建/编辑 Cron 的 API；禁止直接修改 schedule_definitions 或 occurrences。

## Decide Whether to Use Scheduler

The primary reason to define tasks through Scheduler is **UI observability for business administrators**. Developers define schedules and execution logic in code. Business administrators use the task list and detail pages the application builds on the Scheduler API to view schedules, track individual executions, and enable or disable tasks when authorized.

Tasks that do not need to be viewed and tracked through the UI must not be managed by the Scheduler plugin. Give recurring ones a `ScheduleExecutor` of their own from the application's jobs service (`jobExecutorServiceToken` in `@nocobase/app-server/jobs`), and one-off background work a `JobExecutor`; the application development Skill describes both. For administrator-visible tasks, deliver Client registration, access permissions, meaningful task names, and accurate final execution status together; successful server-side dispatch alone is incomplete.

## Then Choose the Execution Model

After deciding to use Scheduler, choose according to business complexity:

| Scenario                                                                                  | Choice          | Implementation boundary                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Periodic cleanup, cache refresh, a single report, or one business Service call            | Own target type | Short operations may complete directly; hand lengthy work to a `JobExecutor`                             |
| A simple operation that happens hourly                                                    | Own target type | Cron alone is not a reason to introduce a heavier execution system                                       |
| Immediate asynchronous execution                                                          | `JobExecutor`   | No Cron Schedule is needed                                                                               |
| A one-time delay, such as ten minutes after a request                                     | Queue `delay`   | No Cron Schedule is needed; `JobExecutor` has no delay                                                   |
| Another execution system with its own references, status queries, and completion protocol | Own target type | The same `registerTarget()` call, with `inspect()` and completion reporting for work that finishes later |

Every schedule points at a registered target; `registerTarget()` is the target extension surface, and there is no built-in target type. A target does not guarantee exactly-once external business effects; design business idempotency.

## Read by Task

- To create or change a schedule, read [Definitions, Registration, and Synchronization](references/definitions.md).
- To register or review a target, hand its work to a `JobExecutor`, report completion, or diagnose a historical occurrence after retargeting, read [Target Extensions and Execution Protocol](references/targets.md).
- To integrate or review the UI/API and permissions, interpret status, or diagnose missing definitions, read [Operations and Verification](references/operations.md), including the complete management route table, separate status dimensions, and application identity checks.

## Development Loop

1. Establish which tasks and execution records administrators need to see. Check Server/Client/CLI registration, page permissions, the application's `jobs` configuration (a `redis` adapter for more than one instance; `memory` serves one process) and `JobExecutorServiceProvider` composition and target availability, then choose or register the target type.
2. Implement business logic and Providers in application source, and call `schedulerServiceToken.defineSchedule(definition)` with an application-wide stable key from that Provider's `register()`/`boot()`.
3. Validate payload/input, timezone, idempotency, and the full asynchronous chain: submission, actual job execution, terminal notification, and recovery from persisted execution state. Obtain credentials through secure business Service configuration, never `target.config`.
4. Run application type checks, relevant tests, and build. Synchronize definitions and, in development, use an administrator account to find the task in the UI and track a real execution to its final state. Confirm the business result.
5. Report the schedule key, execution model, timezone, validation evidence, and unverified runtime boundaries. Use `--finalize` in production only when the complete manifest is visible.

Schedules are defined in code. There is no management API for creating or editing Cron definitions, but the UI and API support enabling and disabling tasks. Do not modify `schedule_definitions`, `schedule_occurrences`, or the schedule backend's rules (BullMQ job schedulers, or the memory adapter's state file) directly, and do not add jobs under Scheduler's `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler` scope.

export function dataResponse(schema, description = 'Success.') {
    return {
        description,
        content: {
            'application/json': {
                schema: composeSchema({ data: schema }, ({ data }) => ({
                    type: 'object',
                    required: ['data'],
                    properties: { data: data },
                })),
            },
        },
    };
}
/**
 * A JSON list response, `{ data: [...], meta }`. `itemSchema` describes one element; `metaSchema` defaults to the
 * shared `ApiListMeta` (`total`, `page`, `pageSize`, `nextPageToken`).
 */
export function listResponse(itemSchema, metaSchema, description = 'Success.') {
    return {
        description,
        content: {
            'application/json': {
                schema: composeSchema({
                    item: itemSchema,
                    meta: metaSchema ?? { $ref: '#/components/schemas/ApiListMeta' },
                }, ({ item, meta }) => ({
                    type: 'object',
                    required: ['data', 'meta'],
                    properties: {
                        data: { type: 'array', items: item },
                        meta: meta,
                    },
                })),
            },
        },
    };
}
/** A response without a body, such as a `204`. */
export function emptyResponse(description = 'No content.') {
    return { description };
}
/**
 * The shared error response for one HTTP status, in the standard error body. Without `description` it refers to the
 * shared component; with one it describes when this route answers that status.
 */
export function apiErrorResponse(code, description) {
    if (description === undefined) {
        return { $ref: `#/components/responses/${apiErrorResponseNames[code]}` };
    }
    return {
        description,
        content: {
            'application/json': {
                schema: { $ref: '#/components/schemas/ApiErrorBody' },
            },
        },
    };
}

/**

 * `addUndeclaredApiRoute`. Each is a defect the API document check reports.

export function findUndeclaredApiRoutes(source, prefix = '/api') {
    return inspectApiRoutes(source, prefix)
        .filter((route) => !route.declared)
        .map(({ method, path }) => ({ method, path }));
}

### API documentation

The running application serves an OpenAPI 3.1 document of every `/api` route it has — its own, every registered plugin's, the data endpoints and Better Auth's `/api/auth/...` — at `<origin><APP_BASE_PATH>/api/swagger` (JSON), with Swagger UI at `<origin><APP_BASE_PATH>/api/swagger/docs`. Locally that is `http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/api/swagger/docs`, with `/main` replaced by the actual `APP_BASE_PATH`.

Reading either needs a signed-in session or an API key: open the Swagger UI in a browser where you are signed in, or send a key in `x-api-key`. A signed-in user creates keys with `POST <APP_BASE_PATH>/api/apiKeys`; the `nocobase-app-plugin-api-keys` Skill covers the plugin. Without a valid credential the routes answer `401` with reason `API_DOCS_UNAUTHENTICATED`, and an application with no access check registered, such as one without the authentication plugin, answers `404`.

```bash
curl -H "x-api-key: <key>" http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/api/swagger
```

**Learn the available endpoints from this document instead of reading route sources.** Before calling an endpoint or building on one, fetch the JSON with a key the user gives you and find the operations by `tags` or `operationId`; read the source only for what the document does not say. Fetch it again after registering a plugin or changing a Collection.

Every `/api` route the application writes declares itself, as in the example above: `tags`, an English `summary` and a unique camelCase `operationId`; input through `apiValidator()`; responses through `dataResponse()`, `listResponse()`, `emptyResponse()` and `apiErrorResponse(status)` for exactly the statuses the route can produce. Do not list `400` for input validation: a route that uses `apiValidator` gets the `400` automatically. List `400` yourself only for another reason, such as a failed precondition. `apiErrorResponses` is `401`, `403` and `500`, for an authenticated route with a permission check; otherwise list each status the route can return with `apiErrorResponse(code)`. Response schemas live in `server/routes/schemas.ts`, typed against what the service returns. A route reached without a credential adds `security: []`. Hide a route with `describeRoute({ hide: true })` and a comment only for the few reasons `references/http-api.md` lists. Data endpoints are documented without a declaration; a field a data exposure adds to every record goes in its `computedFields`. A test starting the application expects `findUndeclaredApiRoutes()` and `findApiDocumentSchemaProblems()` to be empty; `.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md` shows it.

apiValidator(target, schema) 在入参非法时抛 invalidApiInputError，并把 schema 挂为 toJSONSchema/toOpenAPISchema，使校验与文档同源。

export function apiValidator(target, schema, options) {
    const middleware = validator(target, schema, (result) => {
        if (!result.success)
            throw invalidApiInputError(result.error);
    }, options);
    // Document the schema the way the response helpers do, rather than with hono-openapi's own conversion.
    const metadata = middleware[uniqueSymbol];
    const input = resolver(schema, 'input');
    metadata.toJSONSchema = input.toJSONSchema;
    metadata.toOpenAPISchema = async () => {
        const converted = await convertApiSchema(schema, 'input');
        return target === 'header' ? withoutIgnoredHeaders(converted) : converted;
    };
    return middleware;
}
function isStandardSchema(schema) {
    return typeof schema === 'object' && schema !== null && '~standard' in schema;
}

## 2. Migrations and seeds

A migration creates structure. A seed inserts the records the application needs in order to run. Neither does the other's job. Sample or demonstration content is not install data: declare it as a sample seed, described below, never as a plain seed.

```ts

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({

E15 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:147–147

sample seed 仅在安装时 app.sampleData=true 时运行，否则记为 skipped；pnpm nocobase db sample 可在开发中补跑。

Sample data goes in the same `seeds` directory, declared with `defineSeed({ name, sample: true, run })`. It runs only when the application installs its database with `app.sampleData` set (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`): the connection held no migration or seed history before that start, or `db reset` rebuilt it. Otherwise the seed is recorded as skipped and never runs on its own; `pnpm nocobase db sample` runs every skipped sample seed in development, and a deployment refuses it. Sample data that has to go through other plugins' services, such as projects with issues, is registered instead on `sampleDataToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/sample-data`, from a service provider's `boot()`: `container.resolve(sampleDataToken).register({ name, packageName, run })`. It runs once the application is ready, under the same condition, and is recorded in the default connection's seed history as `sample-data:<name>`. The seed history's `status` column, `executed` or `skipped`, records which happened; the library adds it to an existing history table on the next run.

    });
}
function freezeNavigationRoutes(routes) {
    return Object.freeze(routes.map((route) => Object.freeze({
        ...route,
        ...(route.navigation
            ? { navigation: Object.freeze({ ...route.navigation }) }
            : {}),
        ...(route.children
            ? { children: freezeNavigationRoutes(route.children) }
            : {}),
    })));
}
function isDevelopment() {
    return !import.meta.env?.PROD;
}

  async expireOverdue(now: Date = new Date()): Promise<ExpireTodosResult> {
    const { updatedCount } = await this.database
      .repository<TodoRecord>('todos')
      .updateMany({
        filter: (filter) =>
          filter.and([
            filter.boolean('completed').isFalse(),
            filter.boolean('expired').isFalse(),
            filter.date('dueAt').before(now),
          ]),
        values: { expired: true },
      });
    return { updatedCount };
  }

import { todoServiceToken, type TodoService } from '../providers/todos.js';
import { TodoSchema } from './schemas.js';

/** The page resource every todo route checks, the same id `client/routes.ts` declares for the page. */
export const TODO_ACCESS_RESOURCE = 'todos';

/** The `domain` of this application's errors, so a client can branch on `reason` within a known namespace. */
export const TODO_ERROR_DOMAIN = 'todos';

const tags = ['Todos'];
const accessDescription =
  'Requires the `page:todos/access` grant; without it the route answers 403 `TODO_ACCESS_REQUIRED`.';

export const apiRoutes: AppApiRouteContribution<Application> = defineApiRoutes(
  (app) => {
    const router = new Hono();
    // An isolated sub-router so the middleware below covers only `/api/todos` and cannot leak into a contribution
    // mounted later.
    const todosRoutes = new Hono<AuthorizationEnv>();
    const authentication = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);
    const authorization = app.container.resolve(authorizationToken);

    // Render this application's errors in the standard body even when the router is mounted on its own.
    todosRoutes.onError((error, context) => apiErrorHandler(error, context));

    // This route owns its own security: it authenticates the caller and checks the page grant itself rather than
    // relying on another route's middleware or on registration order.
    todosRoutes.use('*', authentication.required(), authorization.middleware());
    todosRoutes.use('*', async (context, next) => {
      const allowed = await context.get('authz').can({
        resource: { type: 'page', id: TODO_ACCESS_RESOURCE },
        action: 'access',
      });
      if (!allowed) {
        throw new ApiError({
          status: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
          reason: 'TODO_ACCESS_REQUIRED',
          domain: TODO_ERROR_DOMAIN,
          message: 'Todo access is required.',
        });
      }
      await next();
    });

    todosRoutes.get(
      '/',
      describeRoute({
        tags,
        summary: 'List todos',
        operationId: 'listTodos',
        description: `Every todo, earliest due time first. ${accessDescription}`,
        responses: {
          200: listResponse(TodoSchema),
          ...apiErrorResponses,
        },
      }),
      async (context) => {
        const todos = app.container.resolve<TodoService>(todoServiceToken);
        return context.json({ data: await todos.list() });
      },
    );

    router.route('/todos', todosRoutes);
    return router;
  },
);

import { defineSeed, type SeedDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';

/** The subset of a `todos` row this seed writes. */
interface TodoRow {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  dueAt: string | Date;
  completed: boolean;
  expired: boolean;
}

const DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

/**
 * The three todos every installation starts with: one already past its due time, one far in the future, and one
 * finished. They are the fixture the `todos.check-expired` schedule is observed against, so they are required data
 * rather than optional sample data: with `sample: true` the seeder records itself as skipped when the database is
 * installed without `APP_SAMPLE_DATA`, and the running application then has nothing to mark or to leave alone.
 *
 * `expired` starts `false` on every row. Pre-setting the flag would leave nothing for the schedule to show; the
 * schedule's own run is the only thing that may flip it. The insert is skipped when a todo with the same title already
 * exists, so a repeated `pnpm nocobase db apply` never adds a duplicate.
 */
const seed: SeedDefinition = defineSeed({
  name: '202610070001_default_todos',

  async run(context) {
    const todos = context.repository<TodoRow>('todos');
    const samples: Pick<
      TodoRow,
      'title' | 'dueAt' | 'completed' | 'expired'
    >[] = [
      {
        title: '已过期待办',
        dueAt: new Date(Date.now() - DAY_MS),
        completed: false,
        expired: false,
      },
      {
        title: '未来待办',
        dueAt: new Date(Date.now() + 3650 * DAY_MS),
        completed: false,
        expired: false,
      },
      {
        title: '已完成待办',
        dueAt: new Date(Date.now() - DAY_MS),
        completed: true,
        expired: false,
      },
    ];

    for (const sample of samples) {
      if (await todos.exists({ filter: { title: sample.title } })) continue;
      await todos.createOne({ values: sample });
    }
  },
});

export default seed;

  }>();

    const controller = new AbortController();

    api
      .request<{ data: Schedule[] }>({
        path: 'scheduler/schedules',
        signal: controller.signal,
      })
      .then(
        ({ data }) => {
          if (!controller.signal.aborted) setState({ key, rows: data });
        },
        (error: unknown) => {
          if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
            setState((previous) => ({ ...previous, key, error }));
          }
        },
      );
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [api, reloadCount]);

  const reload = useCallback(() => setReloadCount((count) => count + 1), []);

  const setEnabled = useCallback(
    async (schedule: Schedule, enabled: boolean): Promise<void> => {
      setPendingId(schedule.id);
      try {
        const { data } = await api.request<{ data: Schedule }>({
          method: 'POST',
          path: `scheduler/schedules/${schedule.id}/${enabled ? 'enable' : 'disable'}`,
        });
        setState((previous) =>
          previous?.rows
            ? {
                ...previous,
                rows: previous.rows.map((row) =>
                  row.id === data.id ? data : row,
                ),
              }
            : previous,

A server test gets an application started the way `pnpm start` starts it — its own runtime, providers and plugins — on isolated test databases of its own, on the dialect `NOCOBASE_TEST_DB_DIALECT` selects, and SQLite when it is unset. An application's and a plugin's tests depend on this package alone: everything `@nocobase/db-testing` offers comes through `./server`, and everything `@nocobase/app-cli/testing` offers comes through `./cli`.\n\n| Entry                          | What it gives a test                                                                                         |\n| ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |\n| `@nocobase/app-testing/server` | `createTestApp()`, the Vitest fixture `createAppTest()`, `createTestAppConfig()`, and `@nocobase/db-testing` |\n| `@nocobase/app-testing/cli`    | `bindTestAppCommand()`, `createTestAppConfig()`, and `@nocobase/app-cli/testing`                             |\n| `@nocobase/app-testing/client` | `renderWithApp()`, which renders a page inside a started client application                                  |\n\nDeclare it in `devDependencies`. A connection not named keeps what the application configures, which is how an `external` connection keeps the database it reads but does not own. `config` adds further sections to the same file, such as `{ users: { initialAdmin: … } }`. The application installs itself on start — its own and its plugins' migrations and seeds on every provisioned connection — unless `install: false`. Its storage goes to a temporary directory, so applications started side by side share no queue or jobs state.\n\nThe application has to load the configuration file it is given, as the application templates' `server/config.ts` does with `context.configPath ?? context.environment.APP_CONFIG_FILE`.\n\nTo act as a signed-in user, sign in through the application with `signIn()` from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing`; a test application qualifies as its target.\n\n```ts\nimport {\n  DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS,\n  signIn,\n} from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing';\n\nconst admin = await signIn(app, DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS);\nconst users = await admin.fetch('/users');\n```\n\n## With Vitest\n\n```ts\nimport { createAppTest } from '@nocobase/app-testing/server';\nimport { createStandaloneServer } from '../../server/standalone.ts';\n\nconst test = createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer });\n\ntest('serves the seeded items', async ({ request, expectCollection }) => {\n  await expectCollection('items').toHaveField('name');\n  expect((await request('/items')).status).toBe(200);\n});\n```\n\nThe context carries `testApp`, `app`, `fetch`, `request`, `database`, `connection` and `expectCollection`. `scope: 'file'` (the default) starts one application for the test file, because starting one installs every migration and seed of it and its plugins; `scope: 'test'` starts a fresh one, on fresh databases, for every test. Everything else — `createDatabaseTest()`, `describeMigration()`, `expectCollection()`, `testDatabaseCapabilities()` — is `@nocobase/db-testing`'s, documented in its README.\n\n## Commands\n\n```ts\nimport {\n  bindTestAppCommand,\n  createTestAppConfig,\n  runAppCommand,\n} from '@nocobase/app-testing/cli';\n\nconst config = await createTestAppConfig();\ntry {\n  const run = await runAppCommand(\n    bindTestAppCommand(MyCommand, { rootDir, config }),\n    ['--json'],\n  );\n  expect(run.json()).toMatchObject({ ok: true });\n} finally {\n  await config.dispose();\n}\n```\n\n`bindTestAppCommand()` is `bindAppCommand()` with the application the command opens pointed at the test databases `config` names, so a command that opens the application — `db apply`, or one of a plugin's — runs against databases of its own.\n\n## Pages\n\n```tsx\nimport { answerApi, renderWithApp } from '@nocobase/app-testing/client';\nimport { screen } from '@testing-library/react';\nimport orders from '../client/index.js';\nimport { OrdersPage } from '../client/pages/orders.js';\n\ntest('lists the orders', async () => {\n  await renderWithApp(<OrdersPage />, {\n    plugins: [orders()],\n    namespace: '@my-scope/app-plugin-orders',\n    route: '/orders',\n    fetch: answerApi(({ method, path }) =>\n      method === 'GET' && path === 'orders'\n        ? { data: [{ name: 'Order 1' }] }\n        : new Response(null, { status: 404 }),\n    ),\n  });\n\n  expect(await screen.findByText('Order 1')).toBeInTheDocument();\n});\n```\n\n`renderWithApp()` starts a client application with `plugins`, their services and their translations, and renders the page inside it under a `MemoryRouter` starting at `route`, the way the application renders it: `useApiClient()`, `useService()`, `useToaster()` and `useTranslation()` are the real ones, not mocks of `@nocobase/app-client`. `namespace` is the translation scope the application gives a plugin's own pages. Translations are strict, so a key the locale files lack fails the test. Call it inside a test: the application shuts down when the test finishes.\n\nThe API client talks to `server` or `fetch`. Pass an application from `createTestApp()` as `server` to reach it in process, with `cookie` set to a session's `cookie` from `signIn()` to act as that user; otherwise `fetch` answers each request, and a request nothing answers fails the call that sent it. The server's `publicBasePath` is the mount path the page sees: the client configuration is written into the document the way the server renders it, so `resolveAppUrl()` builds URLs under it, as it does in the running application. `resolveAssetUrl()` follows that same mount path unless Vite's build-time `BASE_URL` is an absolute HTTP(S) CDN prefix; it is for shipped static files, not API or page URLs. Changing the test server's `publicBasePath` does not change a compiled CDN prefix. `answerApi(handler)` builds that `fetch` from a function of `{ method, path, query, json }`, with `path` below the API root: it returns the JSON body, or a `Response` for another status, and a handler that throws fails the request with status 500 and its message, as does a request whose JSON body cannot be parsed. A `vi.fn()` handler records every call the page made, for `toHaveBeenCalledWith`. `services` registers stand-ins before the plugins start, such as another plugin's client service; leave out the plugin a stand-in replaces. Toasts render after the page as plain text, and `toasts()` on the result lists the open ones.\n\nThe entry loads nothing from `@nocobase/db-testing`, so it runs under jsdom. Rendering Refine under a test's router needs `@refinedev/react-router` inlined, which the React preset of `@nocobase/dev-config` does.\n"}],"details":{}}}

Business plugins consuming Scheduler use `peerDependencies` and import the original Tokens rather than creating same-named Tokens. Installing a package does not enable it. Preserve existing registrations and add the default export of `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/server` to `server/plugins.ts`. Register `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/client` in `client/plugins.ts`; it contributes no pages, only the locales for the titles its server registers with the authorization catalog. Administrator UI observability comes from the application's own schedule pages built on the API. The synchronization command requires `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/cli` in `cli/plugins.ts`.\n\nScheduler keeps its rules on a `ScheduleExecutor` of the application's jobs service, under the `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler` scope, so the application composes `JobExecutorServiceProvider`. `scheduler.jobs` (`SCHEDULER_JOBS`) names the `jobs` configuration it runs on; left out, it follows `jobs.default`, and a name `jobs` does not define stops the application from starting. The `memory` adapter serves one process; more than one instance needs `redis`, which fires each rule once. Keep that configuration's `attempts` at 1: a failed dispatch is recorded as the occurrence's outcome. Complete application migrations before synchronization. Scheduler runs only its own executor; the executors its targets hand work to belong to the targets' owners.\n\nThere is no Server declaration field for schedules — nothing to add to `defineServerPlugin()`, and no conventional file path to discover. A schedule is registered imperatively, wherever the application or a plugin needs it, by resolving `schedulerServiceToken` and calling `defineSchedule(definition)`. Do this from a Provider's `register()` or `boot()`, guarded the same way a target registration is guarded, since the scheduler plugin might not be installed:\n\n```ts\nimport { schedulerServiceToken } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/server/tokens';\nimport type { AppPluginApplication } from '@nocobase/app-server/plugins';\nimport { ServiceProvider } from '@nocobase/service-provider';\n\nexport default class MaintenanceScheduleProvider extends ServiceProvider<AppPluginApplication> {\n  public readonly name = 'app/maintenance-schedule';\n\n  public override async boot(): Promise<void> {\n    if (!this.app.container.has(schedulerServiceToken)) return;\n    this.app.container.resolve(schedulerServiceToken).defineSchedule({\n      key: 'daily-maintenance',\n      title: 'Daily maintenance',\n      schedule: { cron: '0 2 * * *', timezone: 'Asia/Shanghai' },\n      target: {\n        type: 'app.maintenance',\n        config: { batchSize: 100 },\n      },\n    });\n  }\n}\n```\n\nAdd this Provider to the existing `server/providers/index.ts` array; a business plugin's own Provider belongs in its `serviceProviders` contribution instead. `key` is an application-wide stable identifier and forms the schedule's persistent identity together with the application name. Renaming it is not an in-place update, so use a namespaced key such as `sales.daily-report` when the application contains multiple business modules.\n\nBoth `register()` and `boot()` run, across every plugin, before the scheduler's own `start()` reads what was registered — so call order between plugins does not matter, only that the call happens before `start()`. A call after that point has no effect until the next sync.\n\n## Declare a Registered Target\n\nRegister one call per schedule, in whichever Provider owns it:\n\n```ts\nscheduler.defineSchedule({\n  key: 'daily-maintenance',\n  title: 'Daily maintenance',\n  schedule: { cron: '0 2 * * *', timezone: 'Asia/Shanghai' },\n  target: {\n    type: 'app.maintenance',\n    config: { batchSize: 100 },\n  },\n});\n```\n\nImplement and register this target first. Keep only the definitions the application needs.\n\n| Field                   | Current contract                                                                                                                                                           |\n| ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `key`                   | Required; matches `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]*$`; unique within the application. Application name and key form persistent identity, so renaming is not an in-place update |\n| `title` / `description` | Required title / optional description                                                                                                                                      |\n| `schedule.cron`         | Five fields starting with minutes, or six starting with seconds; `*/5 * * * *` runs every five minutes, `*/10 * * * * *` every ten seconds                                 |\n| `schedule.timezone`     | Defaults to `UTC`; use an explicit IANA timezone and consider daylight saving changes for local business time                                                              |\n| `schedule.from` / `to`  | Optional inclusive `Date` boundaries; construct from ISO timestamps with explicit timezone offsets; from must not exceed to                                                |\n| `schedule.limit`        | Optional positive integer; counts firings started, not successful completions. Disabling and re-enabling or changing the definition continues from the firings already run |\n| `target.type`           | A target type the application or a plugin registered. There is no built-in type                                                                                            |\n| `target.config`         | JSON object; no functions, Service instances, or credentials. Sensitive field names are rejected recursively                                                               |\n\n`defineSchedule()` validates and normalizes the definition before adding it to the in-memory manifest for the next sync. It defaults the timezone, computes a definition hash, and freezes the stored definition. Do not construct the hash manually or mutate a definition after passing it in. There are no `enabled`, `retry`, or `overlap` declaration fields; do not insert job or queue options, or fields from another scheduling framework.\n\n`target.config` is whatever the target's own `validate()` accepts. Synchronization checks that the type is registered and runs that `validate()`; anything it does not check is checked when the schedule fires. Successful synchronization does not prove the config drives correct business behavior.\n\n## Synchronization and Deployment\n\nRun from the target application root:\n\n```bash\npnpm nocobase scheduler sync --json\n```\n\nSuccess returns the command envelope with `ok: true`, `status: \"success\"` and `result: { finalize: false }`; also inspect the exit code for failures. The command starts the application for synchronization, writes the rules, and shuts it down, without starting Scheduler's schedule worker. Normal application startup also performs non-destructive synchronization before starting the worker. On the `memory` jobs adapter a running application overwrites what the command wrote when it stops. That loses nothing: every start synchronizes again from the code, and removes again the rules of disabled definitions and of those `--finalize` deactivated.\n\nNormal synchronization validates the complete loaded manifest and upserts definitions without deactivating missing ones. It preserves existing administrator enable/disable settings. During production deployment, once the complete manifest is available, run once per application against the built `dist/`:\n\n```bash\nnode dist/cli/index.js scheduler sync --finalize --json\n```\n\nA built `dist/` has no `.bin`, so run its entry as shown, from any directory, or run `pnpm nocobase scheduler sync --finalize --json` inside `dist/`. Finalization additionally soft-deactivates definitions removed from code, preserving history. Never finalize from a process that loads only some plugins. Import, validation, or write failures must not commit partial reconciliation. Finalization and temporary administrator disabling are distinct operations.\n"}],"details":{}}}

{"type":"tool_execution_update","toolCallId":"call_rmg5qug0","toolName":"bash","args":{"command":"agent-browser skills get core 2>&1 | head -200","timeout":60},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"---\nname: core\ndescription: Core agent-browser usage guide. Read this before running any agent-browser commands. Covers the snapshot-and-ref workflow, navigating pages, interacting with elements (click, fill, type, select), extracting text and data, taking screenshots, managing tabs, handling forms and auth, waiting for content, running multiple browser sessions in parallel, and troubleshooting common failures. Use when the user asks to interact with a website, fill a form, click something, extract data, take a screenshot, log into a site, test a web app, or automate any browser task.\nallowed-tools: Bash(agent-browser:*), Bash(npx agent-browser:*)\n---\n\n# agent-browser core\n\nFast browser automation CLI for AI agents. Chrome/Chromium via CDP, no Playwright or Puppeteer dependency. Accessibility-tree snapshots with compact `@eN` refs let agents interact with pages in ~200-400 tokens instead of parsing raw HTML.\n\nMost normal web tasks (navigate, read, click, fill, extract, screenshot) are covered here. Load a specialized skill when the task falls outside browser web pages — see [When to load another skill](#when-to-load-another-skill).\n\n## The core loop\n\n```bash\nagent-browser open <url>        # 1. Open a page\nagent-browser snapshot -i       # 2. See what's on it (interactive elements only)\nagent-browser click @e3         # 3. Act on refs from the snapshot\nagent-browser snapshot -i       # 4. Re-snapshot after any page change\n```\n\nRefs (`@e1`, `@e2`, ...) are assigned fresh on every snapshot. They become **stale the moment the page changes** — after clicks that navigate, form submits, dynamic re-renders, dialog opens. Always re-snapshot before your next ref interaction.\n\n## Always use your own session\n\nBefore your first command, set a named session for the whole task:\n\n```bash\nexport AGENT_BROWSER_SESSION=\"$(agent-browser session id --scope worktree --prefix task)\"\n```\n\nThe default (unnamed) session is a single shared browser: it is shared with every other agent on the machine and it persists across conversations, so working in it can hijack another agent's page mid-task or navigate away from something the human left open. Every example below assumes a named session is active. See [Run multiple browsers in parallel](#run-multiple-browsers-in-parallel) and `references/session-management.md`.\n\n## Quickstart\n\n```bash\n# Install once\nnpm i -g agent-browser && agent-browser install\n\n# Linux hosts can install required browser libraries too\nagent-browser install --with-deps\n\n# Take a screenshot of a page\nagent-browser open https://example.com\nagent-browser screenshot home.png\nagent-browser close\n\n# Search, click a result, and capture it\nagent-browser open https://duckduckgo.com\nagent-browser snapshot -i                      # find the search box ref\nagent-browser fill @e1 \"agent-browser cli\"\nagent-browser press Enter\nagent-browser wait --load networkidle\nagent-browser snapshot -i                      # refs now reflect results\nagent-browser click @e5                        # click a result\nagent-browser screenshot result.png\n```\n\nThe browser stays running across commands so these feel like a single session. By default, an inactive daemon saves configured restore state, closes its headless browser, and exits after one hour; the next command starts it again. Without `--restore` or another restore key, shutdown discards transient browser state and open tabs. Dashboard mouse, keyboard, and touch input count as activity. Headed browsers, Safari and iOS WebDriver sessions, and user-attached browsers are exempt from the default; provider-owned cloud browsers are not. Use `--idle-timeout <time>` or `AGENT_BROWSER_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` to tune the timeout, and use `0` to disable it. Still run `agent-browser close` (or `close --all`) when you're done.\n\n## MCP integration\n\nFor tools that support Model Context Protocol servers, start the stdio server:\n\n```bash\nagent-browser mcp\nagent-browser mcp --tools all\nagent-browser mcp --tools core,network,react\n```\n\nConfigure the MCP client to launch `agent-browser` with `[\"mcp\"]`. The server defaults to MCP protocol 2025-11-25 and accepts older supported client protocol versions during initialization. The default tools profile is `core`, which keeps MCP context small for everyday browser automation. Use `--tools all` for the full typed CLI parity surface, or combine profiles with commas, such as `--tools core,network,react`. Profiles are `core`, `network`, `state`, `debug`, `tabs`, `react`, `mobile`, and `all`; the `debug` profile includes accessibility audits, plugin registry, and command.run tools. Each tool accepts typed arguments plus `extraArgs` for advanced CLI flags and exact CLI parity. The common `allowedDomains` array maps to `--allowed-domains` and activates the same WebRTC containment and launch-mode restrictions, while `idleTimeout` maps to `--idle-timeout`. Tool discovery is paginated and includes read-only/open-world annotations so modern MCP clients can load the large typed surface incrementally. Use the tool `session` argument or `AGENT_BROWSER_SESSION` to isolate browser sessions.\n\n## eve agent integration\n\nFor eve agents, mount the `@agent-browser/eve` extension instead of hand-writing browser tools. It adds namespaced tools such as `browser__navigate`, `browser__snapshot`, `browser__click`, `browser__fill`, `browser__find`, and `browser__screenshot`, all backed by agent-browser running inside the eve sandbox. The sandbox bootstrap helpers (`installAgentBrowser`, `agentBrowserRevalidationKey`) ship with the same package under `@agent-browser/eve/sandbox`, so `agent/sandbox.ts` needs no extra dependency.\n\n## Reading a page\n\n```bash\nagent-browser snapshot                    # full tree (verbose)\nagent-browser snapshot -i                 # interactive elements only (preferred)\nagent-browser snapshot -i -u              # include href urls on links\nagent-browser snapshot -i -c              # compact (no empty structural nodes)\nagent-browser snapshot -i -d 3            # cap depth at 3 levels\nagent-browser snapshot -s \"#main\"         # scope to a CSS selector\nagent-browser snapshot -i --json          # machine-readable output\n```\n\nSnapshot output looks like:\n\n```\nPage: Example - Log in\nURL: https://example.com/login\n\n@e1 [heading] \"Log in\"\n@e2 [form]\n  @e3 [input type=\"email\"] placeholder=\"Email\"\n  @e4 [input type=\"password\"] placeholder=\"Password\"\n  @e5 [button type=\"submit\"] \"Continue\"\n  @e6 [link] \"Forgot password?\"\n```\n\nFor unstructured reading (no refs needed):\n\n```bash\nagent-browser read                         # read rendered active-tab DOM\nagent-browser read https://docs.example.com/guide  # docs-friendly fetch, prefers markdown\nagent-browser read https://docs.example.com/guide --filter auth  # one matching section\nagent-browser read https://docs.example.com/guide --outline  # compact page headings\nagent-browser read https://docs.example.com --llms index --filter auth  # compact llms.txt discovery\nagent-browser get text @e1                # visible text of an element\nagent-browser get html @e1                # innerHTML\nagent-browser get attr @e1 href           # any attribute\nagent-browser get value @e1               # input value\nagent-browser get title                   # page title\nagent-browser get url                     # current URL\nagent-browser get count \".item\"           # count matching elements\n```\n\nUse `read [url]` when you need to consume documentation or other text pages rather than interact with a rendered UI. Omit the URL to read the rendered DOM of the active tab in the current browser session, including browser auth state and client-side updates. Explicit URL reads send `Accept: text/markdown`, try the same URL with `.md

E52 · app/AGENTS.md:200–202

AGENTS.md Database 段：schema 变更走 migration，应用运行所需数据用 seed；Sample data is not required data，声明为 defineSeed({name, sample:true, run}) 或注册 sampleDataToken，二者仅在以 app.sampleData(APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true) 安装时加载，否则记为 skipped；仅定义，无『任务/验收要求运行时可见』的判定示例。

### Database

Schema changes are migrations under `database/main/migrations/`. Data the application requires to run is a seed under `database/main/seeds/`. Seeds never create structure. Sample data is not required data: it is a seed declared with `defineSeed({ name, sample: true, run })` in the same directory, or, when it has to go through plugins' services, a registration on `sampleDataToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/sample-data`. Either loads only when the database is installed with `app.sampleData` set (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`) and is recorded as skipped otherwise; `pnpm nocobase db sample` loads what was skipped, in development.

原文件 SHA-256：03b54a8d615f57144db20921ce6657030440a6577f498fc1c4a21b170d266774

Business delivery context

业务交付与验收背景

管理员通过既有 Schedules/Settings 页验证“检查过期待办”计划的真实调度与配置：过期待办被标记为已过期，未来与已完成记录不变，重复执行不新增待办，停用配置刷新后持久保存。

What was delivered

业务场景与交付说明

真实调度执行记录

计划 todos.check-expired 处于启用与受控短周期，Schedules 的 View executions 显示 Succeeded 结果，其中一次 Marked 1 todo expired，重复执行为 Marked 0。

过期标记规则正确

仅到期且未完成的“已过期待办”Expired=Yes；2036 到期的“未来待办”与 Completed=Yes 的“已完成待办”均保持 Expired=No。

重复执行幂等

同一目标多次执行后 Todos 仍为 3 条记录，未新增重复待办，状态保持正确。

启用状态持久化

在既有 Settings 页停用计划后刷新，开关保持关闭，Schedules 状态同步为 Disabled。

01管理员登录进入业务界面
→
02Schedules 查看计划与执行记录
→
03启用计划并等待受控短周期调度
→
04核对仅过期待办被标记
→
05再次执行确认待办数量不变
→
06Settings 停用计划
→
07刷新验证启用状态保存

业务顺序示意，操作证据见逐项验收。

01

Todos 实际数据：已过期待办（到期且未完成）Expired=Yes；已完成待办（Completed=Yes）Expired=No；未来待办（2036 到期）Expired=No，只有到期且未完成的记录被标记

A1 · 5 条操作 · 4 条观察 · 4 张证据
Agent 报告通过

原始验收要求

启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

实际操作

  1. 以 nocobase 管理员登录 http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/ 并进入业务界面
  2. 打开 Settings 页确认计划 检查过期待办 处于启用状态
  3. 在 Schedules 页点击 View executions 查看真实执行记录
  4. 重新启用计划后在受控短周期内等待并刷新执行记录，确认新增调度执行
  5. 打开 Todos 页核对三条记录的实际状态

观察结果

  1. 计划 检查过期待办 (todos.check-expired) 在 Settings 与 Schedules 页均显示 Enabled
  2. 执行记录显示真实调度结果：2026-10-16 22:32:06 Succeeded / Marked 1 todo(s) expired，其余为 Succeeded / Marked 0 todo(s) expired
  3. 重新启用（关闭后再开启）后执行记录新增 22:34:20、22:34:30、22:34:40、22:34:50 的 Succeeded / Marked 0 记录，证明调度按短周期真实执行并写入执行记录
  4. Todos 实际数据：已过期待办（到期且未完成）Expired=Yes；已完成待办（Completed=Yes）Expired=No；未来待办（2036 到期）Expired=No，只有到期且未完成的记录被标记

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

02

再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

状态保持正确：已过期待办 Expired=Yes、已完成待办 Completed=Yes/Expired=No、未来待办 Expired=No

A2 · 3 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 2 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

实际操作

  1. 在计划启用状态下继续等待受控短周期调度
  2. 在 Schedules > View executions 中刷新，确认同一目标 todos.check-expired 再次执行
  3. 刷新 Todos 页统计记录总数并核对每行状态

观察结果

  1. 执行记录新增 2026-10-16 22:35:00、22:35:10、22:35:20 的 Succeeded / Marked 0 todo(s) expired，均为同一目标 todos.check-expired 的重复执行
  2. Todos 表仍为 3 条记录（DOM 行数 = 3），重复执行未新增任何待办
  3. 状态保持正确：已过期待办 Expired=Yes、已完成待办 Completed=Yes/Expired=No、未来待办 Expired=No

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

03

在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。

本项仅验证停用配置刷新后持久保存，未声称已验证长期停用或长期无执行

A3 · 4 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 2 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。

实际操作

  1. 打开既有 Settings 设置页，确认计划当前为启用
  2. 点击 检查过期待办 的启用开关并在确认对话框中选择 Disable
  3. 重新加载（刷新）Settings 设置页并核对开关状态
  4. 打开 Schedules 页核对计划状态标签

观察结果

  1. 停用后设置页开关为关闭（checked=false），重新刷新页面后仍为关闭，配置持久化正确
  2. Schedules 页状态由 Enabled 变为 Disabled，与设置页一致
  3. 本项仅验证停用配置刷新后持久保存，未声称已验证长期停用或长期无执行

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

完整原始验收要求
B01. 启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

B02. 再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

B03. 在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。

无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。
